Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a nucleus algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 353. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 505/5.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by nucleus generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 353, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 505. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The nucleus operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 417. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 365 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the nucleus decomposition. By minimality, the element a^417 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 365-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
